25: ## 0. Statement
26: 
27: **Theorem.** Let X be a continuous ME(≤3) law; its density f satisfies f ≥ 0, ∫f = 1 and ∫(1+t²)f < ∞. Then
28: SCV(X) = m₀m₂/m₁² − 1 ≥ v_cos, where v_cos := min_{ω>0, φ∈ℝ} SCV(e^{−t}(1 − cos(ωt − φ))). Moreover:
29: * the minimum is attained in the cos family (case D1);
30: * densities with only real exponents have SCV ≥ Δ₃ > 553/2000;
31: * every density in a strictly-dominant-real-pole cone (case D2) has SCV > v_cos.
32: 
33: Imports:
34: * finite-dimensional convexity, the mean value theorem, Taylor's theorem with remainder, and power series of log;
35: * the correctness of FLINT 3.6.0 / Arb outward-rounded ball arithmetic;
36: * the python-flint 0.9.0 conversion semantics listed in §6.
37: 
38: ## 1. Reductions imported (`reductions/proof/reductions-v2.md`, sha bd497389…1e3f; `reductions/STATUS-v2.md`, sha a644d430…d27e)
39: 
40: Reductions v1 (sha ad6c5091…5536) is kept. Parent audit PASS: `review/me3-parent-primal-reductions-v1/RESULT-v1.json`,
41: sha 9a72e3e7133de30301785af96604b31838b9726d94055717291f3111744c65e3, run x01-red-checks.
42: 
43: * **L1–L2.**
44:   * Domain: measurable f ≥ 0 with 0 < ∫f and ∫(1+t²)f < ∞ (positive-mass hypothesis; Remark 2.3a shows every
45:     application satisfies it).
46:   * Continuous ME(≤3) densities are the normalized nonzero elements of the cones K_E (|E| ≤ 3).
47:   * SCV is scale- and time-scale invariant.
48:   * SCV/(1+SCV) = min_θ R_θ, with R_θ(f) = ∫(t−θ)²f/(θ²∫f).
49:   * Cone minima are attained on extreme rays.
50:   * Assembly: inf over ME(≤3) = min(I_R, I_1, I_2).
51:   * Inclusion K_E ⊆ K_{E′}.
52: * **L3–L4, exact.** Real exponents give SCV ≥ Δ₃, where Δ₃ is the root in [1/4, 1/3] of
53:   h(y) = 32y³ − 144y² + 135y − 27.
54:   * h′(y) = 96(y − 3/2)² − 81 ≥ 149/3 > 0 on [1/4, 1/3].
55:   * h(553/2000) = −1293623/250000000 < 0, hence Δ₃ > 553/2000 = 0.2765.
56:   * Verification is exact (Fractions) in `reductions/runs/exact-certs-v1/exact_certs.json`, sha b18d5163…0a04: 47/47
57:     checks, with wrong controls failing.
58: * **L5, exact upper separation.** In the D1 moment formula with ω = 1, cos φ = 35/37 and sin φ = 12/37:
59:   * (m₀, m₁, m₂) = (27, 62, 171)/74 and SCV = 773/3844 < 1007/5000 < 11/50.
60:   * Hence **v_cos ≤ 773/3844 < T = 11/50 and v_cos < 553/2000 < Δ₃**.
61:   * Attainment holds by continuity, the proven uniform tails and phase periodicity (Thm 5.6; the minimizer has
62:     1/4 < ω < 4).
63:   * The Arb point bound U and the lower bound v_cos ≥ 0.20090156350055 are **optional and unused**. The lower bound
64:     is certified twice:
65:     * by the subagent's `ctl_f_d1bnb_v1.py` (sha 508b1488…41b7), with empty-stack completion;
66:     * independently by the parent desk (RESULT-v1 run d1b-lower-worker-L).
67:   * The contract-band check of `ctl_c_vcos_v2.py` is EXPLORATORY: it tests the Newton value, and its STOP only prints.
68: * **L6.** In D2 (real pole 1, pair 1 + ξ and 1 + ξ̄, ξ = δ − iω, δ, ω > 0, r = δ/ω):
69:   * the extreme rays are e^{−t}h_τ with φ = ωτ ∈ [0, θ₁(r)];
70:   * θ₁ is C¹ and strictly decreasing, from 2π to π;
71:   * m_k = k!(1 − Re(cζ^{k+1})), with c = (1 − ir)e^{(r−i)φ} and ζ = 1/(1 + δ − iω).
72: 
73: Constants: T = 11/50, κ = 1/(1+T) = 50/61 and ρ = T/(1+T). By L5, v_cos ≤ 773/3844 < T.

75: ## 2. Lemma D (unchanged from v3 §2)
76: 
77: Every f ∈ K = cone{e^{−t}, e^{−(1+ξ)t}, e^{−(1+ξ̄)t}} with f ≥ 0 has the form f = e^{−t}H with
78: H = a + Re(ce^{−ξt}) ≥ 0 and a ≥ 0. Let Q_T(y) = (κy² − 2y + 2)/y³ > 0 for y > 0.
79: 
80: **Lemma D.** Suppose that for every θ > 0, W(θ) := Q_T(θ(1+ξ))/Q_T(θ) equals some finite sum Σλ_k e^{−ξt_k} with
81: λ_k ≥ 0, Σλ_k ≤ 1 and t_k ≥ 0. Then SCV ≥ T on K∖0.
82: 
83: *Proof.* As in v3: ∫q_θ f = θ³Q_T(θ)[a + Re(cW)], and a + Re(cW) = (1 − Σλ_k)a + Σλ_k H(t_k) ≥ 0. ∎
84: 
85: Certificate forms:
86: * RADIAL: there is an argument α ≥ 0 of W with |W| ≤ e^{−δα/ω}.
87: * SMALL: |W| ≤ e^{−2πδ/ω}.
88: * TRIANGLE: W = a′ + b′e^{−ξt′} with a′, b′ ≥ 0, a′ + b′ ≤ 1 and t′ ≥ 0.
89: 
90: Hostile review verdict: Lemma D and all three certificates are valid, including the zero cases.
91: 
92: Parametrization: s = θ/(1+θ), W = zz(w₁ + zz(w₂ + zz·w₃)) with zz = 1/(1+ξ).
93: 
94: ## 3. Lemma M (unchanged from v3 §3; review verdict: valid)
95: 
96: Chart P = [0, 3] × [1/5, 40] × [0, 31416/5000] in (r, ω, φ). Run c03 tiles P into 945,680 boxes:
97: * INFEAS: 46,344;
98: * SGE (S > T): 891,318;
99: * DPOS (a₀, a₁, a₂ > 0 and ∂_r S > 0): 8,018.
100: 
101: **Lemma M.** Every feasible (r₀, ω₀, φ₀) ∈ P with r₀ > 0 has S > v_cos.
102: 
103: *Proof.* If S ≥ T > v_cos we are done. Otherwise put σ(r) = S(r, ω₀, φ₀) on [0, r₀].
104: 1. The path stays feasible and inside P, because θ₁ is decreasing.
105: 2. Let r* = inf{r : σ < T on [r, r₀]}. On (r*, r₀], every covering leaf is DPOS, so σ is strictly increasing there.
106: 3. r* > 0 would force σ(r*) ≥ T > σ(r₀) ≥ σ(r*), a contradiction. So r* = 0.
107: 4. Hence σ(r₀) > σ(r₀/2) ≥ σ(0) ≥ v_cos. ∎

147: ## 5. Assembly
148: 
149: Let X be a continuous ME(≤3) law.
150: * Real exponents: SCV ≥ Δ₃ > 553/2000 > 773/3844 ≥ v_cos.
151: * Otherwise, after time scaling, X lies in a D cone.
152:   * D1: SCV ≥ v_cos by definition.
153:   * D2 with dual-assigned poles: SCV ≥ T > 773/3844 ≥ v_cos.
154:   * The remaining D2 cones (ω ∈ [1/5, 40], r ≤ 3): the cone minimum is attained on an extreme ray (L2), and every
155:     extreme ray has S > v_cos (Lemma M).
156: * Attainment: L5. ∎

16: ## 0. Statement
17: 
18: **Theorem.** Every continuous X ∈ ME(≤3) satisfies SCV(X) = m₀m₂/m₁² − 1 ≥ v_cos, where
19: v_cos := min_{ω>0, φ∈ℝ} SCV(e^{−t}(1 − cos(ωt − φ))). The minimum is attained in the cos family (case D1).
20: Densities with only real exponents have SCV ≥ Δ₃ > 0.2765. Every density in a strictly-dominant-real-pole cone
21: (case D2) has SCV > v_cos.
22: 
23: **Proof architecture.**
24: * Written reductions (§1, imported from `reductions/`; proved by the subagent `ME3Reductions` and re-read here).
25: * Two elementary written lemmas: Lemma D (§2) and the Lemma M continuation argument (§3).
26: * Seven certified ball-arithmetic computations over compact charts that together cover every D2 pole
27:   configuration. Each has an exact-rational leaf record and an independent geometric coverage audit (§4).
28:

30: series of log, and the correctness of Arb ball arithmetic (python-flint 0.9.0, 128-bit working precision).

32: ## 1. Reductions imported
33: 
34: Source: `reductions/proof/reductions-v1.md`, sha ad6c5091da11c6f236e24d7fb0dc389b2ef99045b50141411d62dc748dd45536,
35: together with `reductions/STATUS.md`, sha 61776330…2c76, which holds the run hashes.
36: 
37: * **L1–L2.**
38:   * Continuous ME(≤3) densities are exactly the normalized nonzero elements of the cones K_E of nonnegative
39:     exponential polynomials with admissible exponent multisets |E| ≤ 3.
40:   * SCV is invariant under scaling and time scaling.
41:   * SCV/(1+SCV) = min_θ R_θ, with R_θ(f) = ∫(t−θ)²f/(θ²∫f) (Lemma 2.2).
42:   * The infimum over K_E∖0 is attained on an extreme ray (Lemma 2.4).
43:   * Assembly (Cor. 2.6): the infimum over ME(≤3) equals min(I_R, I_1, I_2).
44:   * Lower-order laws are covered by the inclusion K_E ⊆ K_{E′} (Lemma 2.5). The outline's "face" claim is false.
45: * **L3–L4.** With real exponents, SCV ≥ Δ₃ ∈ (0.2765825307809456, 0.2765825307809457). An exact identity gives
46:   Δ₃ > 1/4.
47: * **L5.**
48:   * The D1 extreme rays are the cos family, so I_1 = v_cos.
49:   * The minimum is attained (Thm 5.6).
50:   * Certified bounds: 0.20090156350055 ≤ v_cos < U = 0.20090156350055695880138068… (runs ctl-c-v2, ctl-f-main-v1).
51: * **L6.** In D2 (real pole 1, pair 1 + ξ and 1 + ξ̄, ξ = δ − iω, δ, ω > 0, r = δ/ω):
52:   * the extreme rays are e^{−t}h_τ, with φ = ωτ ∈ [0, θ₁(r)];
53:   * θ₁ is C¹ and strictly decreasing, from 2π (r → 0⁺) to π (r → ∞);
54:   * the moments are m_k = k!(1 − Re(cζ^{k+1})), with c = (1 − ir)e^{(r−i)φ} and ζ = 1/(1 + δ − iω).
55: 
56: Fixed constants: T := 11/50, κ := 1/(1+T) = 50/61, ρ := T/(1+T) = 1 − κ. Note that v_cos < U < T.
57: 
58: ## 2. Lemma D (elementary dual sufficient condition)
59: 
60: Fix δ ≥ 0 and ω > 0, and let ξ = δ − iω. Every f in the cone K with exponents {−1, −(1+ξ), −(1+ξ̄)} has the form
61: f = e^{−t}H with H(t) = a + Re(ce^{−ξt}) ≥ 0 on [0, ∞), and a ≥ 0. (If δ > 0, let t → ∞. If δ = 0, average H over
62: one period.) Let Q_T(y) := (κy² − 2y + 2)/y³. Then Q_T(y) > 0 for y > 0, because κ > 1/2.
63: 
64: **Lemma D.** Suppose that for every θ > 0, W(θ) := Q_T(θ(1+ξ))/Q_T(θ) is a finite combination Σλ_k e^{−ξt_k}
65: with λ_k ≥ 0, Σλ_k ≤ 1 and t_k ≥ 0. Then SCV(f) ≥ T for every f ∈ K∖0.
66: 
67: *Proof.*
68: 1. With q_θ(t) := (t−θ)² − ρθ², we have ∫₀^∞ q_θ e^{−zt} dt = θ³Q_T(θz) for Re z > 0. Hence
69:    ∫q_θ f = θ³Q_T(θ)·[a + Re(cW)].
70: 2. Now a + Re(cW) = (1 − Σλ_k)a + Σλ_k H(t_k) ≥ 0.
71: 3. Therefore R_θ(f) ≥ ρ for every θ, and Lemma 2.2 gives SCV ≥ T. ∎
72: 
73: Certificate forms (each is an explicit finite combination; no closure or limit is used, and the weight on 0 is
74: justified by a ≥ 0):
75: * RADIAL: there is an argument α ≥ 0 of W, of any branch, with |W| ≤ e^{−δα/ω}. Take t = α/ω and
76:   λ = |W|e^{δα/ω} ≤ 1. W = 0 is trivially admissible. If g is a continuous logarithm of W along a path and
77:   Im g ≥ 0, then α = Im g is admissible, and the condition reads Φ := ω Re g + δ Im g ≤ 0.
78: * SMALL: |W| ≤ e^{−2πδ/ω}. This is RADIAL with α = Arg₊ ∈ [0, 2π).
79: * TRIANGLE: W = a′ + b′e^{−ξt′}, with a′, b′ ≥ 0, a′ + b′ ≤ 1 and t′ ≥ 0.
80: 
81: Parametrization. Put s = θ/(1+θ) ∈ (0, 1), N₁ = κs² − 2s(1−s) + 2(1−s)² > 0 and zz = 1/(1+ξ). Then
82: W = zz(w₁ + zz(w₂ + zz·w₃)), with w₁ = κs²/N₁, w₂ = −2s(1−s)/N₁ and w₃ = 2(1−s)²/N₁. (Multiply N(θz)/(N(θ)z³) by
83: (1−s)².) Boxes may contain s ∈ {0, 1}; their enclosures still contain the true values for every s ∈ (0, 1).
84: 
85: ## 3. Lemma M and the continuation argument (primal chart P)
86: 
87: Let R_P := [0, 3] × [1/5, 40] × [0, 31416/5000] in (r, ω, φ). For feasible points let S(r, ω, φ) be the SCV of the
88: extreme ray: e^{−t}h_{φ/ω} if r > 0, and the D1 ray if r = 0. The closed-form moments are analytic in (r, ω, φ),
89: including at r = 0. With a_k = m_k/k!:
90: * 1 + S = 2a₀a₂/a₁²;
91: * ∂_r log(1+S) = D/(a₀a₁a₂), where D := a₀′a₁a₂ + a₀a₁a₂′ − 2a₀a₁′a₂;
92: * a_k′ = −Re(cζ^{k+1}[φ + (r−i)/(1+r²) − (k+1)ωζ]), an exact sympy identity (run v01).
93: 
94: **Certificate.**
95: * Run c03 used spec `code/spec_primal_P_v1.json`, sha 6339c775…759c, frozen before the run. It tiles R_P into
96:   945,680 closed boxes, each of which is INFEAS (no feasible point: φ > θ₁(r) everywhere), SGE (S > T everywhere)
97:   or DPOS (a₀, a₁, a₂ > 0 and ∂_r S > 0 everywhere).
98: * Counts: SGE 891,318; DPOS 8,018; INFEAS 46,344; FAIL 0.
99: * Independent audit a01 replays the split rule from geometry alone: tiling_ok.
100: * The DPOS leaves lie in r ≤ 0.2344, ω ∈ [0.5887, 1.6614] and φ ≤ 0.7363 (bounding box).
101: 
102: **Lemma M.** For every feasible (r₀, ω₀, φ₀) ∈ R_P with r₀ > 0, S(r₀, ω₀, φ₀) > v_cos.
103: 
104: *Proof.*
105: 1. If S(r₀, ω₀, φ₀) ≥ T we are done, since T > v_cos. Otherwise put σ(r) := S(r, ω₀, φ₀) on [0, r₀]. These points
106:    are feasible: for 0 < r ≤ r₀ we have φ₀ ≤ θ₁(r₀) ≤ θ₁(r) because θ₁ is decreasing, and r = 0 is D1. They also
107:    lie in R_P. The function σ is continuous on [0, r₀] and C¹ on (0, r₀].
108: 2. Let J := {r : σ < T on [r, r₀]} and r* := inf J.
109: 3. For r ∈ (r*, r₀], any leaf box containing (r, ω₀, φ₀) contains a feasible point with S < T. So it is neither
110:    INFEAS nor SGE, hence it is DPOS, and σ′(r) > 0. Thus σ is strictly increasing on (r*, r₀].
111: 4. If r* > 0, continuity gives σ(r*) ≥ T, while monotonicity gives σ(r*) ≤ σ(r₀) < T, a contradiction. So
112:    r* = 0, and σ(r₀) > σ(0) = SCV(e^{−t}(1 − cos(ω₀t − φ₀))) ≥ v_cos. ∎
113: 
114: ## 4. Far region: six dual charts, all certified
115: 
116: The specification is `proof/charts-spec-v1.md` (sha 5a43c252…1ff1), together with `code/spec_dual_v1.json`
117: (sha 23dd1dad…f6c1; receipt `receipts/spec-dual-freeze-v1.json`, sha 69f60dc5…40c4, frozen at 04:41:52) and, for
118: SWC only, `code/spec_dual_v2.json` (sha d9b490ef…8930; receipt `receipts/spec-dual-freeze-v2.json`, sha
119: 60c540ad…cfe1, frozen at 04:50:47).
120: 
121: **Implementation change, SWC chart.**
122: * The corner series coefficients use p = (s(1−s)(1 + i√(2κ−1)) − κs²)/N₁. This equals the spec text's
123:   (−A + i√(4B − A²))/2, because 2Re p = −A and |p|² = B. It avoids a square root of a ball whose radicand 4B − A²
124:   suffered cancellation near s = 1.
125: * The failing calibration attempts are kept: k04 (spec root) and k03 (linear recurrence).
126: * Library `code/me3dual_v8.py`, sha 5f3e7675…d1ea; driver `code/cert_dual_v6.py`, sha abffeefd…7339.
127: * The other dual charts ran `code/cert_dual_v5.py` + `code/me3dual_v7.py`. Their tests are those of
128:   `me3dual_v6.py`/`v4.py`/`v3.py`, as listed in the spec.

152: point (s, δ, ω) of its box, one of RADIAL, SMALL or TRIANGLE (the justifications are in charts-spec-v1.md). By
153: Lemma D, every density with poles in a dual-assigned configuration has SCV ≥ T > v_cos.
154: 
155: ## 5. Assembly
156: 
157: Let X be a continuous ME(≤3) law.
158: * Real exponents: SCV ≥ Δ₃ > U > v_cos.
159: * Otherwise, after time scaling, X lies in a D cone (δ ≥ 0, ω > 0).
160:   * D1 (δ = 0): SCV ≥ v_cos by definition.
161:   * D2 configurations in a dual-assigned set: SCV ≥ T > v_cos.
162:   * The remaining D2 configurations (ω ∈ [1/5, 40], r ≤ 3): by L2 the cone minimum is attained on an extreme ray,
163:     and by Lemma M it exceeds v_cos.
164: * Attainment: L5 Theorem 5.6. ∎
165: 
166: ## 6. Correctness dependencies and limits
167: 
168: * **Certified computations.** They rely on four things: Arb's outward-correct ball arithmetic; the code implementing
169:   the stated formulas (the derivative is checked by an exact sympy identity, v01, and the moments by 40-digit
170:   quadrature, v01); the deterministic split rule; and the frozen specs.
171: * **Coverage.** Each leaf record is audited by code that does not evaluate any test. The parent will rebuild the
172:   evaluators from the definitions.
173: * **Data.** About 101 MB of leaf records (all compressed).
174: * **Not done.** No Lean or kernel check, and no human referee. This is not the book's general question.
175: 
176: ## 7. Controls and replays
177: 
178: * **Negative controls, run n01** (`runs/n01-controls/controls.json`, sha 3ea93340…88a6). Each fails for the intended
179:   reason:
180:   * M1: the primal chart without DPOS FAILs near the D1 optimum (1024 FAIL boxes; S < T there).
181:   * M2: with the derivative sign flipped, it FAILs.
182:   * M3: the dual GENERAL test FAILs inside the valley.
183:   * M4: the corner chart with T′ = 3/10 > Δ₃ FAILs.
184:   * M5: the INFEAS predicate never fires on boxes that contain feasible points.
185:   * M6: the same small primal region with DPOS is certified.
186: * **Audit mutants, n02/n03.** A dropped leaf, a FAIL label, a flipped path bit and a false SKIP are each rejected
187:   by `check_cover_v2.py`.
188: * **Independent replay by ME3Reductions.** Final status: `reductions/replay/REPLAY-STATUS-v2.md`, sha
189:   af02cc293f26722a8b7304aac5f305d3e173af766e892fef4e83493247b514fd. This is a point-evaluation smoke test (mpmath,
190:   50 digits), not a certificate. The evaluators were written from the definitions (reductions-v1 Lemmas 6.4–6.6;
191:   the Lemma D statement and the charts-spec-v1 maps) and import nothing from the lane code. All its runs were
192:   launched through `code/run_v1.sh` with pre/post receipts.
193:   * Primal, 948 sampled leaves (midpoint, 8 corners and 26 extra points each): 0 violations. Minimum margins:
194:     S − T ≥ 0.011273 on SGE leaves (30,409 feasible points); ∂S/∂r ≥ 0.0983 on DPOS leaves; φ − θ₁ ≥ 4.23e−4 on
195:     INFEAS leaves. ∂S/∂r agrees with central differences, and at 24 points the closed-form moments match direct
196:     quadrature to 9e−30.
197:   * Primal, all 945,680 c03 leaves (`runs/rp-primal-full-v2`), using its own box rebuild:
198:     * The rebuild reproduces the 948 sample boxes exactly, and the leaves tile all 61,440 cells.
199:     * SGE: min S − T = 0.0078155 over 861,780 feasible midpoints.
200:     * DPOS: min ∂S/∂r = 0.0801 over 72,162 points.
201:     * INFEAS: each leaf passes at its worst corner (r_lo, φ_lo) at 50 digits; min g − 1 = 1.58e−4.
202:   * Dual samples, 0 violations in every chart. The exact spiral-hull membership test (radial, or chord when
203:     α > θ₁) agrees with a 2880-direction support-function check; every point fell in the radial branch.
204:     | Chart | Boxes | Points | Min margin |
205:     |---|---|---|---|
206:     | G | 1025 + 3 SKIP | 35,875 | 0.0289 |
207:     | SWC | 353 | 12,148 | 6.2e−9 |

210:     | LD1 | 112 | 3,767 | 0.346 |
211:     | LD2 | 1072 + 18 SKIP | 37,324 | 0.0288 |
212:   * All SKIP boxes satisfy their exact skip rules. One G SKIP box is exactly on the P boundary,
213:     δ_hi = 3ω_lo = 51/80, which is allowed because P is closed.
214:   * The SWC margin is tiny at δ = ω → 0, where W → 1. That is the expected corner degeneracy, which the
215:     certified corner series handles analytically. It is not a near-violation.
216:   * Mutation controls: 3 bad primal boxes gave 105/105 violations, and a dual box with |W| = 1.014 was flagged
217:     at 35/35 points. A SKIP box outside P and a FAIL leaf were also reported.

1: # Certified charts for the D2 bound — specification v2
2: 
3: Worker `MathPhaseType3`. v1 (`proof/charts-spec-v1.md`, sha 5a43c252…1ff1, frozen before the certified dual runs) is
4: kept unchanged. v2 makes four changes and nothing else; every other definition is as in v1.
5: 
6: 1. **SWC.** The coefficient implementation is exact: `me3dual_v9.py`, spec `code/spec_dual_v3.json`, run d03-SWC.
7:    This follows hostile review `review/me3-charts-codex-v1.note.md`, finding 1. The SWC run d02 under v1/spec v2 is
8:    invalid.
9: 2. **LD1/LD2 quantifiers.** They are narrowed to the physical domain, the s-union is stated explicitly, and one "iff"
10:    is replaced by a sufficient implication (finding 5).
11: 3. **Coverage checker.** `code/check_cover_v3.py` hard-codes the outcome sets and the domains, rejects FAIL
12:    unconditionally, checks depth, and binds each leaf file to its run summary (finding 4).
13: 4. **Libraries and drivers.** The library and driver versions are restated for every chart.
14: 
15: ## Common conventions (unchanged from v1)
16: 
17: * T = 11/50, κ = 1/(1+T) = 50/61.
18: * Normal form: real pole 1, complex pair 1 + ξ, 1 + ξ̄ with ξ = δ − iω, δ ≥ 0, ω > 0. Ratio r = δ/ω. Extreme-ray
19:   phase φ = ωτ ∈ [0, θ₁(r)].
20: * Arithmetic: python-flint 0.9.0 (FLINT 3.6.0, Arb) at 128 bits. Boxes have exact rational endpoints and enter as
21:   `arb(lo).union(arb(hi))`.
22: * Python-level arithmetic on certified paths is audited in `proof/float-audit-v1.md`: Python integers enter exactly,
23:   and floats appear only in the G-chart TRIANGLE-angle heuristic.
24: * MVF enclosures, the split rule and the FAIL/cap rule are as in v1.
25:

27: 
28: | Chart | Driver | Library chain | Spec | Run | Audit (check_cover_v3) |
29: |---|---|---|---|---|---|

31: | G, SWB, LW, LD1, LD2 | `cert_dual_v5.py` | `me3dual_v7` → `v6` → `v4` / `v3`; `me3jet_v1/v2` | `spec_dual_v1.json` | d01-* | b03-cover-* |
32: | SWC | `cert_dual_v7.py` | `me3dual_v9` (corner code self-contained; `me3jet_v1`) | `spec_dual_v3.json` | d03-SWC | b03-cover-SWC |
33: 
34: ## Chart changes
35: 
36: ### SWC (replaces the v1 SWC paragraph)
37: 
38: g(ξ) = log W = Σ_{j≥1} c_jξ^j for |ξ| < min(1, 1/√B), with
39: 
40:   c_j = (−2 Re(p^j) + 3(−1)^j)/j,  p = (s(1−s)(1 + i√(2κ−1)) − κs²)/N₁.
41: 
42: * This p equals (−A + i√(4B − A²))/2, because 2 Re p = −A and |p|² = B. The cancellation-free form avoids a square
43:   root of a ball.
44: * In the code, the Python integer 3(−1)^j is added exactly to the ball −2 Re(p^j), and the sum is divided by the
45:   Python integer j in ball arithmetic. Every computed c_j ball therefore contains the exact c_j at every box point.
46: * X_m, U_m, Ψ, Ig, the tail bounds and the acceptance tests are as in v1, with N = 20.
47: 
48: ### LD1 and LD2: physical-domain quantifiers (replaces the v1 LD paragraphs and coverage line)
49: 
50: The charts use padded rectangles. A certificate is claimed only for the **physical points** of each box:
51: * LD1: s ∈ [1/16, 1), d = 1/(1+δ) ∈ (0, 1/65] and β = arctan(ω/(1+δ)) ∈ (0, π/2).
52: * LD2: χ ∈ (0, 1), ρ_t > 0 and β ∈ (0, π/2), with s = χρ_t ∈ (0, 1/16] and d = (1−χ)ρ_t / cos β ∈ (0, 1/65].
53: 
54: The points of a padded box with β ≥ π/2, d = 0, s ∈ {0, 1}, ρ_t = 0 or χ = 1 are limiting or nonphysical faces with
55: no pole interpretation. Nothing is claimed there. For example, at s = 1 and β > π/2 the factor d cos β is negative,
56: so β + arg P is not an argument of W.
57: 
58: For every physical point of a CERT leaf:
59: * LD1: ζ = 1/(1+ξ) = d·cos β·e^{iβ}, W = ζP, P = w₁ + w₂ζ + w₃ζ², and δ/ω = (1−d)/tan β > 0.
60: * An accepted candidate α ≥ 0 is an argument of W with |W| ≤ e^{−(1−d)α/tan β} = e^{−δα/ω}. This is the RADIAL
61:   certificate.
62: * Candidates:
63:   * β + (principal arg P), if Re P > 0 on the box and the result is ≥ 0;
64:   * β + Arg₊ P;
65:   * the fallback Arg₊ W ∈ [0, 2π), for which 0 ≤ Arg₊W / tan β ≤ 2π / tan β_l. This remains valid when the box's
66:     upper β endpoint exceeds π/2; physical points still have β < π/2.
67: * **Boxes with β_l = 0.**
68:   * Re P > 0 on the box and arg P(0) = 0, so α(β) = ∫₀^β (1 + Im(P_β/P)) du.
69:   * **If** 1 + inf_box Im(P_β/P) ≥ 0, **then** α ≥ 0. This is a sufficient condition only; v1's "iff", and the same
70:     word in the docstring of the executed `me3dual_v3._ld_ratio_small_beta`, are superseded.
71:   * Also α/tan β ≤ (1 + sup Im(P_β/P))·(β/tan β) ≤ 1 + sup Im(P_β/P).
72: * LD1 bounds 1 − d by its box ball.
73: * LD2 uses P̂ = P/ρ_t², with χ and ρ_t held fixed in P̂_β. It replaces 1 − d by 1, which is conservative because
74:   the physical exponent δα/ω ≥ 0.
75: * LD2 SKIP leaves contain no physical point:
76:   * skip-s: χ_lρ_{t,l} > 1/16, exact.
77:   * skip-d: (1−χ_h)ρ_{t,l} > cos(β_l)/65, ball comparison. Since d cos β = (1−χ)ρ_t ≥ (1−χ_h)ρ_{t,l} and cos is
78:     decreasing on [0, π], every point of the box has d > 1/65 or cos β ≤ 0.
79: 
80: **s-coverage is by the union, not by either chart alone.** For δ ≥ 64 (d ∈ (0, 1/65]):
81: * every s ∈ [1/16, 1) is a physical point of LD1;
82: * every s ∈ (0, 1/16] is a physical point of LD2, because ρ_t = s + d cos β ∈ (0, 1/16 + 1/65] = (0, 81/1040] and
83:   χ = s/ρ_t ∈ (0, 1).
84: * So LD1 ∪ LD2 covers every s ∈ (0, 1), that is, every test centre μ = s/(1−s) ∈ (0, ∞).
85: 
86: ## Coverage of all D2 pole configurations (ξ = δ − iω, δ > 0, ω > 0; replaces the v1 coverage list)
87: 
88: * δ ≥ 64: LD1 ∪ LD2, physical points, s ∈ (0, 1).
89: * δ < 64 and ω ≥ 640: LW (ν = 1/ω ∈ (0, 1/640]), all s ∈ [0, 1].
90: * δ < 64 and ω ∈ [1/5, 640]: P (Lemma M) if ω ≤ 40 and δ ≤ 3ω. Otherwise G, all s ∈ [0, 1], where G's SKIP leaves
91:   lie in P.
92: * δ < 64 and ω < 1/5: SWC if δ ≤ 3/10, otherwise SWB, all s ∈ [0, 1]. The ω = 0 faces are normalized extensions; the
93:   RADIAL conclusion is used only for ω > 0.
94: 
95: Closed boundaries overlap safely (δ = 64, ω = 640, ω = 1/5, δ = 3/10 and ∂P). The faces s ∈ {0, 1} extend the
96: rational formula for W; every finite θ > 0 has s ∈ (0, 1).
97: 
98: ## Coverage audit v3 (replaces the v1 audit sentence)
99: 
100: `code/check_cover_v3.py` performs the following checks. It evaluates no chart test.
101: * **Spec.** For each chart it verifies that the spec's kind, T, variable order (primal: r, ω, φ), outer domain,
102:   parameters and series length equal the hard-coded values above. For LD charts it also certifies β_top > π/2 by a
103:   ball comparison and checks ρ_t top = s_max + d_max and LD1 s_lo = LD2 s_max exactly.
104: * **Outcomes.** Each leaf outcome must lie in the chart's hard-coded set:
105:   * P: SGE, DPOS, INFEAS;
106:   * G: CERT:small, CERT:radial, CERT:radial-mvf, CERT:triangle, SKIP:in-primal-P;
107:   * SWB: CERT:bedge, CERT:bedge-mvf;
108:   * SWC: CERT:corner, CERT:corner-mvf;
109:   * LW: CERT:small;
110:   * LD1: CERT:ld1;
111:   * LD2: CERT:ld2, SKIP:skip-s, SKIP:skip-d.
112: 
113:   FAIL is rejected unconditionally.
114: * **Tree.** Every leaf depth is ≤ the spec's max_depth. Per initial cell, the leaf paths must be exactly the
115:   depth-first leaves of a full binary tree. Every SKIP rule is re-checked.
116: * **Run binding.** The SHA-256 of the uncompressed leaf lines and the outcome counts must equal the run summary's,
117:   and the summary must report certificate_ok with no cap hit.
118: * **Limits.** This is a geometric and bookkeeping audit only. The predicates are checked by replay (the parent's
119:   desks, and ME3Reductions' point replay).

SOURCE 03-charts-spec-v1.md SHA256 5a43c252887b954b69902267f8047b5a06b0edc5c682fc8bfd8b5db49c6d1ff1 ORIGINAL /Users/jinleic/repos/aifours/mathematics/evidence/six-lane-20261016/me3-minimum-scv/proof/charts-spec-v1.md
1: # Certified charts for the D2 bound — frozen specification v1
2: 
3: Worker `MathPhaseType3`. Written before the certified dual runs; the primal chart P was frozen in
4: `code/spec_primal_P_v1.json` before run c03. This document fixes the mathematics of every chart. Each chart's
5: machine-readable parameters (exact rational breakpoints, split weights, depth and evaluation caps) are in the spec
6: JSON named below. Library versions: `code/me3lib_v1.py` and `code/me3jet_v1.py` (primal);
7: `code/me3dual_v7.py` → `me3dual_v6.py` → `me3dual_v4.py` / `me3dual_v3.py`, with jets from `me3jet_v1.py` and
8: `me3jet_v2.py` (dual). Every leaf record has the format `cell path outcome`. The coverage audits
9: `code/check_cover_v1.py` (primal) and `code/check_cover_v2.py` (dual) replay the split rule from geometry alone.
10: 
11: ## Common conventions
12: 
13: * T = 11/50. κ = 1/(1+T) = 50/61. Normal form: real pole 1, complex pair 1 + ξ, 1 + ξ̄ with ξ = δ − iω, δ ≥ 0,
14:   ω > 0. Ratios: r = δ/ω. Extreme-ray phase: φ = ωτ ∈ [0, θ₁(r)] (reductions L6).
15: * Arithmetic: python-flint 0.9.0 (Arb). All boxes have exact rational endpoints and enter as
16:   `arb(lo).union(arb(hi))`. Comparisons are certified ball comparisons. Working precision: 128 bits. A
17:   "mean-value form" (MVF) enclosure of a C¹ function F on a box B with midpoint m and half-widths h is
18:   F(m) + Σ_j ∂_jF(B)·[−h_j, h_j], where the ∂_jF(B) come from first-order jets evaluated on B. It is valid because
19:   F(x) − F(m) = ∫₀¹ ∇F(m + t(x − m))·(x − m) dt and every ∇F value lies in the convex balls ∂_jF(B).
20: * Split rule: bisect, at the exact rational midpoint, the coordinate maximizing width × weight (ties go to the
21:   lowest index). Child 0 is the lower half. Depth-first order. A box still undecided at max depth is a FAIL leaf.
22:   Any FAIL, or hitting the evaluation cap, means the chart is not certified.
23: 
24: ## Chart P (primal; frozen in spec_primal_P_v1.json, run c03, coverage a01)
25: 
26: Domain: r ∈ [0, 3], ω ∈ [1/5, 40], φ ∈ [0, 31416/5000] ⊃ [0, θ₁(r)]. The quantities are the closed-form moments
27: a_k = 1 − Re(c ζ^{k+1}), c = (1 − ir)e^{(r−i)φ}, ζ = 1/(1 + rω − iω) (reductions Lemma 6.6),
28: G = 2a₀a₂ − (1+T)a₁², and D = a₀′a₁a₂ + a₀a₁a₂′ − 2a₀a₁′a₂ with a_k′ = ∂a_k/∂r at fixed (ω, φ)
29: (an exact sympy identity, run v01). Outcomes:
30: * INFEAS: φ_lo > π, r_lo > 0 and g_{r_lo}(φ_lo) > 1. Then φ > θ₁(r) for every box point, since θ₁ is decreasing
31:   and g_r is increasing on (π, 2π).
32: * SGE: a₁ > 0 and G > 0 (naive, else MVF), i.e. S > T at every box point.
33: * DPOS: a₀, a₁, a₂ > 0 and D > 0 (naive, else MVF), i.e. ∂S/∂r > 0 at fixed (ω, φ) at every box point.
34: 
35: ## Dual charts (spec_dual_v1.json; driver cert_dual_v5.py)
36: 
37: For s ∈ [0, 1] (test centre μ = s/(1−s)), zz = 1/(1+ξ) and
38: W = zz(w₁ + zz(w₂ + zz·w₃)), with w₁ = κs²/N₁, w₂ = −2s(1−s)/N₁, w₃ = 2(1−s)²/N₁ and
39: N₁ = κs² − 2s(1−s) + 2(1−s)² > 0. This equals Q_T(μ(1+ξ))/Q_T(μ) for s ∈ [0, 1), where
40: Q_T(y) = (κy² − 2y + 2)/y³ is the Laplace transform of q(t) = (t−1)² − T/(1+T).
41: **Lemma D** (proof/me3-theorem-v1.md §2): if, for every s ∈ (0, 1), W lies in conv({0} ∪ {e^{−ξt} : t ≥ 0}), then
42: every nonnegative density with poles {1, 1+ξ, 1+ξ̄} has SCV ≥ T. Accepted certificates (finite convex combinations):
43: RADIAL (W = |W|e^{iα} with α ≥ 0 and |W| ≤ e^{−δα/ω}), SMALL (|W| ≤ e^{−2πδ/ω}), TRIANGLE
44: (W = a + b·e^{−ξθ′/ω}, a, b ≥ 0, a + b ≤ 1).
45: 
46: | Chart | Variables (exact ranges) | Assigned (δ, ω) set | Test(s) |
47: |---|---|---|---|
48: | SWC (corner) | s ∈ [0,1], δ ∈ [0, 3/10], ω ∈ [0, 1/5] | δ ≤ 3/10, 0 < ω ≤ 1/5 | series: Ψ_N + tail < 0 and Ig_N − tail ≥ 0 |
49: | SWB (B-edge) | s ∈ [0,1], δ ∈ [3/10, 64], ω ∈ [0, 1/5] | 3/10 ≤ δ ≤ 64, 0 < ω ≤ 1/5 | ω-expansion: B + ω_h²C₊ + E < 0, Im g/ω lower bound > 0 |
50: | G (general) | s ∈ [0,1], δ ∈ [0, 64], ω ∈ [1/5, 640] | same, minus P = {ω ∈ [1/5, 40], δ ≤ 3ω} (SKIP leaves) | SMALL / RADIAL / RADIAL-MVF / TRIANGLE |
51: | LW (large ω) | s ∈ [0,1], δ ∈ [0, 64], ν = 1/ω ∈ [0, 1/640] | δ ≤ 64, ω ≥ 640 | SMALL |
52: | LD1 (large δ) | s ∈ [1/16, 1], d = 1/(1+δ) ∈ [0, 1/65], β = arctan(ω/(1+δ)) ∈ [0, 15708/10000] | δ ≥ 64, all ω > 0 | RADIAL with α = β + arg P |
53: | LD2 (large δ, small s) | χ ∈ [0,1], ρ_t ∈ [0, 81/1040], β ∈ [0, 15708/10000]; s = χρ_t, d cos β = (1−χ)ρ_t | δ ≥ 64, all ω > 0, s ≤ 1/16 (SKIP leaves outside) | RADIAL with α = β + arg P̂ |
54: 
55: Chart details:
56: * **SWC.** g(ξ) = log W = Σ_{j≥1} c_jξ^j for |ξ| < min(1, 1/√B), with c_j = −2Re(p^j)/j + 3(−1)^j/j,
57:   p = (−A + i√(4B − A²))/2, A = (2κs² − 2s(1−s))/N₁, B = κs²/N₁ ≤ κ/(κ − 1/2). We have ξ^m = X_m − iωU_m, with
58:   X₀ = 1, U₀ = 0, X_{m+1} = δX_m − ω²U_m and U_{m+1} = X_m + δU_m, and |U_m| ≤ m|ξ|^{m−1}.
59:   * Φ/(ω|ξ|²) = −Σ_{j≥2} c_jU_{j−1} =: Ψ and Im g/ω = −Σ_{j≥1} c_jU_j =: Ig.
60:   * With N explicit terms (N = 20), the tails are bounded by 2ρ²(ρx)^{N−1}/(1 − ρx) + 3x^{N−1}/(1 − x) and
61:     2ρ(ρx)^N/(1 − ρx) + 3x^N/(1 − x), where x = √(δ_h² + ω_h²) and ρ = √(B_up).
62:   * Pass ⇒ Im g ≥ 0, so α = Im g ≥ 0 is an argument of W = e^g, and Φ = ω log|W| + δα < 0, giving RADIAL.
63: * **SWB.** For fixed real δ, put h(y) = g(δ − iy), y ∈ [0, ω]. Taylor's theorem with remainder gives
64:   * Re g(δ−iω) = g − ω²g″/2 + R_a with |R_a| ≤ ω⁴M₄/24;
65:   * Im g(δ−iω) = −ωg′ + ω³g‴/6 + R_b with |R_b| ≤ ω⁵M₅/120;
66:   * so Φ/ω = B + ω²C + E, where B = g − δg′, C = −g″/2 + δg‴/6 and |E| ≤ ω_h⁴(M₄/24 + δ_h M₅/120).
67:   The derivatives of g on the real axis are rational in (s, δ) and computed in real Arb. M_k bounds |g^{(k)}| on the
68:   rectangle [δ_l, δ_h] − i[0, ω_h] via |g^{(k)}| ≤ (k−1)!(|v₁|^k + |v₂|^k + 3|z|^{−k}), with
69:   v_{1,2} = κs/(κsz − (1−s)(1 ± i√(2κ−1))) = 1/(z − z_{1,2}), where z_{1,2} are the zeros of Nt(s, ·).
70:   Pass ⇒ Im g/ω ≥ −g′ − ω_h²|g‴|/6 − ω_h⁴M₅/120 > 0 and Φ/ω < 0, giving RADIAL.
71: * **G.** W is enclosed by the intersection of its naive ball with its complex MVF. The tests are:
72:   * SMALL;
73:   * RADIAL, with the argument branch fixed on the box by Im W > 0 (α = arg W), Im W < 0 (α = arg W + 2π) or
74:     Re W < 0 (α = arg(−W) + π); then ω log|W| + δα < 0, naive or MVF, where the MVF uses jets of log(±W) on the
75:     same branch and requires the midpoint to share it;
76:   * TRIANGLE with θ′ a rational approximation (from below, to 1e−4) of θ₁(r_mid). Its validity does not depend
77:     on θ′: e^{−ξθ′/ω} = e^{−rθ′}e^{iθ′} lies on the spiral for every θ′ ≥ 0.
78:   SKIP: a box with ω_h ≤ 40, ω_l ≥ 1/5 and δ_h ≤ 3ω_l lies in P.
79: * **LW.** zz = ν/(ν(1+δ) − i). SMALL: |W| < e^{−2πδν}.
80: * **LD1.** ζ = 1/(1+ξ) = d·cos β·e^{iβ}, W = ζP, P = w₁ + w₂ζ + w₃ζ², and δ/ω = (1−d)/tan β. Any α ≥ 0 that is an
81:   argument of W works; candidates are β + (principal arg P) when it is ≥ 0, β + Arg₊P, and finally
82:   Arg₊W ∈ [0, 2π) (ratio ≤ 2π/tan β_l).
83:   * For boxes with β_l = 0: Re P > 0 on the box, arg P(0) = 0 and the mean value theorem give
84:     α/tan β ≤ 1 + sup Im(P_β/P), with P_β = (w₂ + 2w₃ζ)·i·d·e^{2iβ}, and α ≥ 0 iff 1 + inf ≥ 0.
85:   * Boxes reaching β = 15708/10000 > π/2 use the fallback.
86:   * Test: |W| < exp(−(1−d)·ratio), with 1 − d taken as the box ball.
87: * **LD2.** P̂ = P/ρ_t² = [κχ² − 2χ(1−χ)(1−s)e^{iβ} + 2(1−s)²(1−χ)²e^{2iβ}]/N₁(s) and W = (1−χ)ρ_t³e^{iβ}P̂.
88:   The same argument choices as LD1 apply, with the factor 1 − d bounded by 1, which is conservative since the
89:   exponent is ≥ 0. SKIP: χ_lρ_{t,l} > 1/16 (s too large) or (1−χ_h)ρ_{t,l} > cos(β_l)/65 (d > 1/65), checked
90:   exactly or in balls.
91: 
92: ## Coverage of all D-type pole configurations (ξ = δ − iω, δ ≥ 0, ω > 0)
93: 
94: * δ ≥ 64: LD1 ∪ LD2 (every s ∈ [0, 1], every β ∈ (0, π/2)).
95: * δ < 64 and ω ≥ 640: LW.
96: * δ < 64 and ω ∈ [1/5, 640]: P (if ω ≤ 40 and δ ≤ 3ω) or G.
97: * δ < 64 and ω < 1/5: SWC (δ ≤ 3/10) or SWB (δ ≥ 3/10).
98: 
99: Each dual chart covers all s ∈ [0, 1], hence all μ ∈ (0, ∞). The audits check that the leaves tile each chart and
100: that every SKIP leaf satisfies its geometric rule.

SOURCE 04-me3dual_v9.py SHA256 77a826c0a8f7773bee1715daa1d092c669893b176fbb15ab6ba6727eb22dd341 ORIGINAL /Users/jinleic/repos/aifours/mathematics/evidence/six-lane-20261016/me3-minimum-scv/code/me3dual_v9.py
1: """DUAL far-region tests, version 9: me3dual_v8.py with EXACT corner-series coefficients.
2: 
3: Defect fixed (hostile review institute/runs/six-lane-2026-10-16/review/me3-charts-codex-v1.note.md, finding 1,
4: FATAL): v8 (like v3 and v7) formed c_j = -2 Re(p^j)/j + 3(-1)^j/j as `-2 * RE(pk) / j + 3 * (-1) ** j / j`. The
5: second summand is a Python int/int true division, i.e. a binary float rounded BEFORE Arb receives it (first inexact
6: at j = 5), so the implemented polynomial did not enclose the stated exact coefficients and the j > N tail bound did
7: not cover the difference. v9 forms
8: 
9:     c_j = (-2 Re(p^j) + 3(-1)^j) / j
10: 
11: with the Python integer 3(-1)^j added exactly to the ball -2 Re(p^j) and a single outward-rounded ball division by
12: the Python integer j (python-flint converts Python integers exactly). Nothing else changes: the root
13: p = (s(1-s)(1 + i c0) - kappa s^2)/N1 with c0 = sqrt(2 kappa - 1), the recurrence for (X_m, U_m), the tail bounds and
14: the acceptance tests are those of me3dual_v8.py. They are copied here verbatim (not imported), so the certified
15: corner path executes no v3/v7/v8 corner code. The other chart tests are re-exported unchanged from me3dual_v7.py;
16: their Python-level arithmetic is covered by the static float audit proof/float-audit-v1.md.
17: """
18: from flint import arb, acb
19: from me3jet_v1 import RE, mvf
20: import me3dual_v3 as V3
21: import me3dual_v7 as V7
22: 
23: ball, sym, N1f = V3.ball, V3.sym, V3.N1f
24: test_general, test_largew, test_ld1, test_ld2, test_bedge = (V7.test_general, V7.test_largew, V7.test_ld1,
25:                                                              V7.test_ld2, V7.test_bedge)
26: 
27: 
28: def corner_series_v9(S, Dl, Om, kap, N):
29:     """Psi_N = -sum_{j=2}^N c_j U_{j-1} and Ig_N = -sum_{j=1}^N c_j U_j, c_j = (-2 Re(p^j) + 3(-1)^j)/j exactly.
30:     Generic over acb balls and first-order jets (me3jet_v1.J)."""
31:     N1 = N1f(S, kap)
32:     c0 = (2 * kap - 1).sqrt()
33:     p = (S * (1 - S) * acb(1, c0) - kap * S * S) / N1
34:     c = [None]
35:     pk = p
36:     for j in range(1, N + 1):
37:         c.append((-2 * RE(pk) + 3 * (-1) ** j) / j)
38:         pk = pk * p
39:     X, U = [1], [0]
40:     w2 = Om * Om
41:     for m in range(N):
42:         X.append(Dl * X[m] - w2 * U[m])
43:         U.append(X[m] + Dl * U[m])
44:     psi = 0
45:     for j in range(2, N + 1):
46:         psi = psi - c[j] * U[j - 1]
47:     ig = 0
48:     for j in range(1, N + 1):
49:         ig = ig - c[j] * U[j]
50:     return psi, ig
51: 
52: 
53: def test_corner(sl, sh, dl, dh, wl, wh, kap, N):
54:     sb, db, wb = ball(sl, sh), ball(dl, dh), ball(wl, wh)
55:     N1 = N1f(sb, kap)
56:     Bup = arb((kap * sb * sb / N1).upper())
57:     rp = Bup.sqrt()
58:     xm = (arb(dh) * arb(dh) + arb(wh) * arb(wh)).sqrt()
59:     q1 = rp * xm
60:     if not (q1 < 1 and xm < 1):
61:         return False, "corner-radius"
62:     tail_psi = 2 * rp * rp * q1 ** (N - 1) / (1 - q1) + 3 * xm ** (N - 1) / (1 - xm)
63:     tail_ig = 2 * rp * q1 ** N / (1 - q1) + 3 * xm ** N / (1 - xm)
64:     psi, ig = corner_series_v9(acb(sb), acb(db), acb(wb), kap, N)
65:     psi, ig = psi.real, ig.real
66:     mids = [arb((sl + sh) / 2), arb((dl + dh) / 2), arb((wl + wh) / 2)]
67:     hb = [sym(sl, sh), sym(dl, dh), sym(wl, wh)]
68:     if not (ig - tail_ig >= 0):
69:         def Fi(S, Dl, Om):
70:             return (corner_series_v9(S, Dl, Om, kap, N)[1],)
71:         (igm,) = mvf(Fi, mids, [sb, db, wb], hb)
72:         ig = ig.intersection(igm) if ig.is_finite() else igm
73:         if not (ig - tail_ig >= 0):
74:             return False, "corner-img"
75:     if psi + tail_psi < 0:
76:         return True, "corner"
77: 
78:     def Fp(S, Dl, Om):
79:         return (corner_series_v9(S, Dl, Om, kap, N)[0],)
80:     (pm,) = mvf(Fp, mids, [sb, db, wb], hb)
81:     if pm + tail_psi < 0:
82:         return True, "corner-mvf"
83:     return False, "corner"

SOURCE 06-cert_dual_v7.py SHA256 72fb546a99828d644226aee7b2824f418e51291f961d222f87ad9d17fad64f3f ORIGINAL /Users/jinleic/repos/aifours/mathematics/evidence/six-lane-20261016/me3-minimum-scv/code/cert_dual_v7.py
1: """Certified branch-and-bound for the DUAL far-region charts, version 7 (frozen spec JSON in argv[2]); identical to
2: cert_dual_v6.py except that the tests come from me3dual_v9.py (exact corner coefficients; v6 imported me3dual_v8).
3: 
4: argv[1] = run directory; argv[2] = spec; argv[3] = chart name. Writes dual.json and leaves.txt.gz (exclusive create).
5: Initial cells: product of the chart's breakpoint intervals (exact rationals); cells numbered with the first
6: variable most significant. Split: argmax_i width_i * weight_i (ties -> lowest index), exact midpoint; child 0 lower.
7: Leaf outcomes: CERT:<test tag> (test passed on the whole box), SKIP:<rule> (box outside the chart's assigned set,
8: covered elsewhere), FAIL (max depth).  Tests: see me3dual_v9.py, me3dual_v7.py, me3dual_v6.py and me3dual_v3.py (shared definitions).
9: """
10: import sys
11: import json
12: import time
13: import gzip
14: import math
15: import hashlib
16: sys.path.insert(0, __file__.rsplit("/", 1)[0])
17: import lane_guard  # noqa: E402
18: import flint  # noqa: E402
19: from flint import arb, fmpq  # noqa: E402
20: import me3dual_v9 as D  # noqa: E402
21: 
22: RUN = sys.argv[1]
23: spec = json.load(open(sys.argv[2]))
24: chart = sys.argv[3]
25: cs = spec["charts"][chart]
26: flint.ctx.prec = spec["prec"]
27: 
28: 
29: def Q(x):
30:     x = str(x)
31:     if "/" in x:
32:         a, b = x.split("/")
33:         return fmpq(int(a), int(b))
34:     return fmpq(int(x))
35: 
36: 
37: T = Q(spec["T"])
38: kap = arb(1 / (1 + T))
39: bps = [[Q(v) for v in dim] for dim in cs["breakpoints"]]
40: wts = [Q(w) for w in cs["split_weights"]]
41: maxdepth = cs["max_depth"]
42: kind = cs["kind"]
43: par = {k: Q(v) for k, v in cs.get("params", {}).items()}
44: 
45: 
46: def theta1_float(r):
47:     if r <= 0:
48:         return 2 * math.pi
49:     lo, hi = math.pi, 2 * math.pi
50:     for _ in range(80):
51:         mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
52:         if math.cos(mid) - r * math.sin(mid) - math.exp(-r * mid) < 0:
53:             lo = mid
54:         else:
55:             hi = mid
56:     return lo
57: 
58: 
59: def evaluate(box):
60:     (al, ah), (bl, bh), (cl, ch) = box
61:     if kind == "C":
62:         return D.test_corner(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap, int(cs["series_terms"]))
63:     if kind == "B":
64:         return D.test_bedge(al, ah, bl, bh, ch, kap)
65:     if kind == "G":
66:         if ch <= par["P_w_hi"] and bh <= par["P_r_hi"] * cl and cl >= par["P_w_lo"]:
67:             return True, "SKIP:in-primal-P"
68:         rm = float((bl + bh) / 2) / float((cl + ch) / 2)
69:         th = theta1_float(rm)
70:         theta_tri = fmpq(int(math.floor(th * 10000)), 10000) if th < 2 * math.pi - 1e-6 else None
71:         return D.test_general(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap, theta_tri)
72:     if kind == "LW":
73:         return D.test_largew(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap)
74:     if kind == "LD1":
75:         return D.test_ld1(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap, par["beta_top"])
76:     if kind == "LD2":
77:         ok, tag = D.test_ld2(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap, par["beta_top"], par["s_max"], par["d_max"])
78:         if ok and tag.startswith("skip"):
79:             return True, "SKIP:" + tag
80:         return ok, tag
81:     raise SystemExit("unknown kind")
82: 
83: 
84: def split_dim(box):
85:     sc = [(box[i][1] - box[i][0]) * wts[i] for i in range(3)]
86:     best = 0
87:     for i in (1, 2):
88:         if sc[i] > sc[best]:
89:             best = i
90:     return best
91: 
92: 
93: t0 = time.time()
94: cells = []
95: for a in range(len(bps[0]) - 1):
96:     for b in range(len(bps[1]) - 1):
97:         for c in range(len(bps[2]) - 1):
98:             cells.append(((bps[0][a], bps[0][a + 1]), (bps[1][b], bps[1][b + 1]), (bps[2][c], bps[2][c + 1])))
99: counts = {}
100: reasons = {}
101: fails = []
102: hasher = hashlib.sha256()
103: gz = gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=lane_guard.xopen(RUN + "/leaves.txt.gz", "xb"), mode="wb", mtime=0)
104: evals = 0
105: capped = False
106: for ci, cell in enumerate(cells):
107:     stack = [(cell, "")]
108:     while stack:
109:         box, path = stack.pop()
110:         ok, tag = evaluate(box)
111:         evals += 1
112:         if ok:
113:             out = tag if tag.startswith("SKIP") else "CERT:" + tag
114:         else:
115:             reasons[tag] = reasons.get(tag, 0) + 1
116:             if len(path) >= maxdepth:
117:                 out = "FAIL"
118:                 if len(fails) < 40:
119:                     fails.append({"cell": ci, "path": path, "box": [[str(x), str(y)] for x, y in box], "why": tag})
120:             else:
121:                 i = split_dim(box)
122:                 lo, hi = box[i]
123:                 mid = (lo + hi) / 2
124:                 b0, b1 = list(box), list(box)
125:                 b0[i], b1[i] = (lo, mid), (mid, hi)
126:                 stack.append((tuple(b1), path + "1"))
127:                 stack.append((tuple(b0), path + "0"))
128:                 continue
129:         counts[out] = counts.get(out, 0) + 1
130:         line = f"{ci} {path if path else '-'} {out}\n"
131:         gz.write(line.encode())
132:         hasher.update(line.encode())
133:         if evals % 200000 == 0:
134:             print("progress", evals, counts, round(time.time() - t0, 1), flush=True)
135:         if evals > cs["eval_cap"]:
136:             capped = True
137:             break
138:     if capped:
139:         print("EVAL CAP REACHED; no conclusion", flush=True)
140:         break
141: gz.close()
142: res = {"spec": spec["name"], "chart": chart, "kind": kind, "counts": counts, "evaluations": evals, "cells": len(cells),
143:        "fail_reasons_before_split": reasons, "fails_first40": fails, "eval_cap_hit": capped,
144:        "leaf_lines_sha256": hasher.hexdigest(), "certificate_ok": ("FAIL" not in counts) and not capped,
145:        "elapsed_seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 2)}
146: with lane_guard.xopen(RUN + "/dual.json") as fh:
147:     json.dump(res, fh, indent=1)
148: print("SUMMARY", json.dumps({k: res[k] for k in ("chart", "counts", "evaluations", "certificate_ok", "elapsed_seconds")}))

1: """Independent coverage audit, version 3, for the primal chart P and the six dual charts (v1/v2 kept unchanged).

4:   * the allowed outcome set of every chart is HARD-CODED below (not read from the command line, not accepted by
5:     prefix); FAIL and every unknown outcome are rejected unconditionally;
6:   * the spec is bound to the proof: chart kind, T, variable order, outer domain (first/last breakpoints or region),
7:     chart parameters and series length must equal the hard-coded values of proof/me3-theorem-v4.md section 4;
8:     LD charts additionally need beta_top > pi/2 (ball comparison) and rho_t top = s_max + d_max (exact);
9:   * every leaf depth is checked against the spec's max_depth;
10:   * the leaf file is bound to the run summary: SHA-256 of the uncompressed leaf lines must equal the summary's
11:     leaf_lines_sha256 and the per-outcome counts must equal the summary's counts (FAIL-free, cap not hit,
12:     certificate_ok true);
13:   * SKIP rules re-checked as in v2 (in-primal-P and skip-s exactly in rationals; skip-d with 128-bit arb balls).
14: Geometry only: no chart test is evaluated. Tiling check as in v1/v2: per initial cell, in file order, the leaf paths
15: must be exactly the depth-first leaves of a full binary tree (no gaps, no overlaps, no extra or missing cells).
16: argv[1] = run dir (output); argv[2] = spec; argv[3] = chart (P, G, SWB, SWC, LW, LD1, LD2); argv[4] = leaves.txt.gz;

18: """

20: import json
21: import gzip
22: import hashlib
23: sys.path.insert(0, __file__.rsplit("/", 1)[0])
24: import lane_guard  # noqa: E402
25: from fractions import Fraction as Fr  # noqa: E402
26: import flint  # noqa: E402
27: from flint import arb, fmpq  # noqa: E402
28: 
29: flint.ctx.prec = 128
30: RUN, specp, chart, leafp, summp, stride = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3], sys.argv[4], sys.argv[5], int(sys.argv[6])
31: 
32: EXPECT = {
33:     "P": {"kind": None, "domain": [("0", "3"), ("1/5", "40"), ("0", "31416/5000")],
34:           "allowed": {"SGE", "DPOS", "INFEAS"}},
35:     "G": {"kind": "G", "domain": [("0", "1"), ("0", "64"), ("1/5", "640")],
36:           "allowed": {"CERT:small", "CERT:radial", "CERT:radial-mvf", "CERT:triangle", "SKIP:in-primal-P"},
37:           "params": {"P_w_lo": "1/5", "P_w_hi": "40", "P_r_hi": "3"}},
38:     "SWB": {"kind": "B", "domain": [("0", "1"), ("3/10", "64"), ("0", "1/5")],
39:             "allowed": {"CERT:bedge", "CERT:bedge-mvf"}},
40:     "SWC": {"kind": "C", "domain": [("0", "1"), ("0", "3/10"), ("0", "1/5")],
41:             "allowed": {"CERT:corner", "CERT:corner-mvf"}, "series_terms": 20},
42:     "LW": {"kind": "LW", "domain": [("0", "1"), ("0", "64"), ("0", "1/640")], "allowed": {"CERT:small"}},
43:     "LD1": {"kind": "LD1", "domain": [("1/16", "1"), ("0", "1/65"), ("0", "15708/10000")],
44:             "allowed": {"CERT:ld1"}, "params": {"beta_top": "15708/10000"}},
45:     "LD2": {"kind": "LD2", "domain": [("0", "1"), ("0", "81/1040"), ("0", "15708/10000")],
46:             "allowed": {"CERT:ld2", "SKIP:skip-s", "SKIP:skip-d"},
47:             "params": {"beta_top": "15708/10000", "s_max": "1/16", "d_max": "1/65"}},
48: }
49: E = EXPECT[chart]
50: spec = json.load(open(specp))
51: summ = json.load(open(summp))
52: errors = []
53: 
54: 
55: def F(x):
56:     return Fr(str(x))
57: 
58: 
59: def fq(x):
60:     return fmpq(x.numerator, x.denominator)
61: 
62: 
63: if F(spec["T"]) != Fr(11, 50):
64:     errors.append(f"spec T {spec['T']} != 11/50")
65: if chart == "P":
66:     names = ("r", "w", "phi")                      # bound to cert_primal_v3.py line 45
67:     reg = [(F(spec["region"][k][0]), F(spec["region"][k][1])) for k in names]
68:     nsplit = [int(spec["initial_split"][k]) for k in names]
69:     bps = [[reg[d][0] + (reg[d][1] - reg[d][0]) * Fr(i, nsplit[d]) for i in range(nsplit[d] + 1)] for d in range(3)]
70:     wts = [F(w) for w in spec["split_weights"]]
71:     maxdepth = int(spec["max_depth"])
72: else:
73:     cs = spec["charts"][chart]
74:     if cs["kind"] != E["kind"]:
75:         errors.append(f"chart kind {cs['kind']} != {E['kind']}")
76:     bps = [[F(v) for v in dim] for dim in cs["breakpoints"]]
77:     wts = [F(w) for w in cs["split_weights"]]
78:     maxdepth = int(cs["max_depth"])
79:     par = {k: F(v) for k, v in cs.get("params", {}).items()}
80:     if par != {k: F(v) for k, v in E.get("params", {}).items()}:
81:         errors.append(f"chart params {cs.get('params')} != {E.get('params')}")

1: """No-deletion / no-temp / no-overwrite guard for this lane (pattern of tz43-gcdcqs18/code/lane_guard.py).
2: 
3: Import FIRST in every script of this lane.
4: * os.remove/os.unlink/os.rmdir/os.removedirs/shutil.rmtree/shutil.move raise (no deletion, no cleanup);
5: * os.replace/os.rename raise (no silent overwrite by rename);
6: * tempfile creators raise (no system temporary artifacts);
7: * xopen(path, mode) opens for writing only if the path does not exist yet (modes 'x'/'xb').
8: """
9: import os
10: import shutil
11: import tempfile
12: 
13: 
14: def _blocked(*args, **kwargs):
15:     raise RuntimeError("blocked by lane_guard: deletion, renaming and system temp files are disabled in this lane")
16: 
17: 
18: os.remove = os.unlink = os.rmdir = os.removedirs = _blocked
19: os.replace = os.rename = _blocked
20: shutil.rmtree = shutil.move = _blocked
21: tempfile.mkstemp = tempfile.mkdtemp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory = _blocked
22: tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile = tempfile.TemporaryFile = tempfile.SpooledTemporaryFile = _blocked
23: 
24: 
25: def xopen(path, mode="x"):
26:     """Exclusive-create open: refuses to overwrite an existing file."""
27:     assert mode in ("x", "xb"), "only exclusive-create modes are allowed"
28:     return open(path, mode)
29: 
30: 
31: def new_dir(path):
32:     """Create a brand-new directory; refuse an existing one (mkdir, exist_ok=False)."""
33:     os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=False)
34:     return path

SOURCE 13-cert_dual_v5.py SHA256 b2fcab98749497b6e682d0b6e42c042cc73671d679a7d3ddfef543eb58aa855d ORIGINAL /Users/jinleic/repos/aifours/mathematics/evidence/six-lane-20261016/me3-minimum-scv/code/cert_dual_v5.py
1: """Certified branch-and-bound for the DUAL far-region charts, version 5 (frozen spec JSON in argv[2]); identical to
2: cert_dual_v2.py except that the tests come from me3dual_v7.py (v2: v3 library; v3 driver: superseded v4; v4 driver: v6).
3: 
4: argv[1] = run directory; argv[2] = spec; argv[3] = chart name. Writes dual.json and leaves.txt.gz (exclusive create).
5: Initial cells: product of the chart's breakpoint intervals (exact rationals); cells numbered with the first
6: variable most significant. Split: argmax_i width_i * weight_i (ties -> lowest index), exact midpoint; child 0 lower.
7: Leaf outcomes: CERT:<test tag> (test passed on the whole box), SKIP:<rule> (box outside the chart's assigned set,
8: covered elsewhere), FAIL (max depth).  Tests: see me3dual_v7.py, me3dual_v6.py and me3dual_v3.py (shared definitions).
9: """
10: import sys
11: import json
12: import time
13: import gzip
14: import math
15: import hashlib
16: sys.path.insert(0, __file__.rsplit("/", 1)[0])
17: import lane_guard  # noqa: E402
18: import flint  # noqa: E402
19: from flint import arb, fmpq  # noqa: E402
20: import me3dual_v7 as D  # noqa: E402
21: 
22: RUN = sys.argv[1]
23: spec = json.load(open(sys.argv[2]))
24: chart = sys.argv[3]
25: cs = spec["charts"][chart]
26: flint.ctx.prec = spec["prec"]
27: 
28: 
29: def Q(x):
30:     x = str(x)
31:     if "/" in x:
32:         a, b = x.split("/")
33:         return fmpq(int(a), int(b))
34:     return fmpq(int(x))
35: 
36: 
37: T = Q(spec["T"])
38: kap = arb(1 / (1 + T))
39: bps = [[Q(v) for v in dim] for dim in cs["breakpoints"]]
40: wts = [Q(w) for w in cs["split_weights"]]
41: maxdepth = cs["max_depth"]
42: kind = cs["kind"]
43: par = {k: Q(v) for k, v in cs.get("params", {}).items()}
44: 
45: 
46: def theta1_float(r):
47:     if r <= 0:
48:         return 2 * math.pi
49:     lo, hi = math.pi, 2 * math.pi
50:     for _ in range(80):
51:         mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
52:         if math.cos(mid) - r * math.sin(mid) - math.exp(-r * mid) < 0:
53:             lo = mid
54:         else:
55:             hi = mid
56:     return lo
57: 
58: 
59: def evaluate(box):
60:     (al, ah), (bl, bh), (cl, ch) = box
61:     if kind == "C":
62:         return D.test_corner(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap, int(cs["series_terms"]))
63:     if kind == "B":
64:         return D.test_bedge(al, ah, bl, bh, ch, kap)
65:     if kind == "G":
66:         if ch <= par["P_w_hi"] and bh <= par["P_r_hi"] * cl and cl >= par["P_w_lo"]:
67:             return True, "SKIP:in-primal-P"
68:         rm = float((bl + bh) / 2) / float((cl + ch) / 2)
69:         th = theta1_float(rm)
70:         theta_tri = fmpq(int(math.floor(th * 10000)), 10000) if th < 2 * math.pi - 1e-6 else None
71:         return D.test_general(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap, theta_tri)
72:     if kind == "LW":
73:         return D.test_largew(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap)
74:     if kind == "LD1":
75:         return D.test_ld1(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap, par["beta_top"])
76:     if kind == "LD2":
77:         ok, tag = D.test_ld2(al, ah, bl, bh, cl, ch, kap, par["beta_top"], par["s_max"], par["d_max"])
78:         if ok and tag.startswith("skip"):
79:             return True, "SKIP:" + tag
80:         return ok, tag
81:     raise SystemExit("unknown kind")
82: 
83: 
84: def split_dim(box):
85:     sc = [(box[i][1] - box[i][0]) * wts[i] for i in range(3)]
86:     best = 0
87:     for i in (1, 2):
88:         if sc[i] > sc[best]:
89:             best = i
90:     return best
91: 
92: 
93: t0 = time.time()
94: cells = []
95: for a in range(len(bps[0]) - 1):
96:     for b in range(len(bps[1]) - 1):
97:         for c in range(len(bps[2]) - 1):
98:             cells.append(((bps[0][a], bps[0][a + 1]), (bps[1][b], bps[1][b + 1]), (bps[2][c], bps[2][c + 1])))
99: counts = {}
100: reasons = {}
101: fails = []
102: hasher = hashlib.sha256()
103: gz = gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=lane_guard.xopen(RUN + "/leaves.txt.gz", "xb"), mode="wb", mtime=0)
104: evals = 0
105: capped = False
106: for ci, cell in enumerate(cells):
107:     stack = [(cell, "")]
108:     while stack:
109:         box, path = stack.pop()
110:         ok, tag = evaluate(box)
111:         evals += 1
112:         if ok:
113:             out = tag if tag.startswith("SKIP") else "CERT:" + tag
114:         else:
115:             reasons[tag] = reasons.get(tag, 0) + 1
116:             if len(path) >= maxdepth:
117:                 out = "FAIL"
118:                 if len(fails) < 40:
119:                     fails.append({"cell": ci, "path": path, "box": [[str(x), str(y)] for x, y in box], "why": tag})
120:             else:
121:                 i = split_dim(box)
122:                 lo, hi = box[i]
123:                 mid = (lo + hi) / 2
124:                 b0, b1 = list(box), list(box)
125:                 b0[i], b1[i] = (lo, mid), (mid, hi)
126:                 stack.append((tuple(b1), path + "1"))
127:                 stack.append((tuple(b0), path + "0"))
128:                 continue
129:         counts[out] = counts.get(out, 0) + 1
130:         line = f"{ci} {path if path else '-'} {out}\n"
131:         gz.write(line.encode())
132:         hasher.update(line.encode())
133:         if evals % 200000 == 0:
134:             print("progress", evals, counts, round(time.time() - t0, 1), flush=True)
135:         if evals > cs["eval_cap"]:
136:             capped = True
137:             break
138:     if capped:
139:         print("EVAL CAP REACHED; no conclusion", flush=True)
140:         break
141: gz.close()
142: res = {"spec": spec["name"], "chart": chart, "kind": kind, "counts": counts, "evaluations": evals, "cells": len(cells),
143:        "fail_reasons_before_split": reasons, "fails_first40": fails, "eval_cap_hit": capped,
144:        "leaf_lines_sha256": hasher.hexdigest(), "certificate_ok": ("FAIL" not in counts) and not capped,
145:        "elapsed_seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 2)}
146: with lane_guard.xopen(RUN + "/dual.json") as fh:
147:     json.dump(res, fh, indent=1)
148: print("SUMMARY", json.dumps({k: res[k] for k in ("chart", "counts", "evaluations", "certificate_ok", "elapsed_seconds")}))

SOURCE 14-me3dual_v3.py SHA256 94ca521ed03b688e050311fe1255e1f47fd40298cc2edb82bfa39da1714774c9 ORIGINAL /Users/jinleic/repos/aifours/mathematics/evidence/six-lane-20261016/me3-minimum-scv/code/me3dual_v3.py
1: """DUAL far-region tests, version 3 (python-flint arb/acb + first-order jets for mean-value forms).
2: 
3: Notation (real pole 1, pair -(1+delta) +- i w, xi = delta - i w, z = 1 + xi), T = 11/50, kappa = 1/(1+T):
4:   Nt(s, z) = kappa s^2 z^2 - 2 s (1-s) z + 2 (1-s)^2,  N1 = Nt(s, 1) > 0,  s = mu/(1+mu) in [0,1],
5:   W(s, xi) = Nt(s, z) / (N1 z^3) = Q_T(mu z)/Q_T(mu),   Q_T(y) = (kappa y^2 - 2 y + 2)/y^3.
6:   W = w1/z + w2/z^2 + w3/z^3 with w1 = kappa s^2/N1, w2 = -2 s(1-s)/N1, w3 = 2(1-s)^2/N1 (w1 + w2 + w3 = 1).
7: LEMMA D (written proof): if for every s in (0,1) the point W lies in conv({0} U {e^{-xi t}: t >= 0}), every
8: nonnegative density with poles {1, z, conj(z)} has SCV >= T. Certificates used here (each a finite convex combination):
9:   RADIAL : W = |W| e^{i alpha} for some alpha >= 0 with |W| <= e^{-delta alpha / w}   [t = alpha/w, weight |W| e^{delta alpha/w}]
10:            (any argument alpha >= 0 of W is allowed; W = 0 is trivially fine);
11:   SMALL  : |W| <= e^{-2 pi delta / w}  (RADIAL with alpha = Arg_+ W in [0, 2 pi));
12:   TRIANGLE: W = a + b e^{-xi t'} with a, b >= 0, a + b <= 1, t' = theta'/w for an explicit rational theta'.
13: g = log W (analytic continuation from g(0) = 0 along segments from xi = 0): Phi := Im[conj(xi) g] = w Re g + delta Im g;
14: if Im g >= 0 then alpha = Im g is an argument of W and RADIAL <=> Phi <= 0.
15: Series (corner): 1 + A xi + B xi^2 = Nt(s, 1+xi)/N1, A = (2 kappa s^2 - 2 s(1-s))/N1, B = kappa s^2/N1;
16:   g = sum_{j>=1} c_j xi^j, c_j = -P_j/j + 3(-1)^j/j, P_0 = 2, P_1 = -A, P_j = -A P_{j-1} - B P_{j-2}
17:   (P_j = p^j + q^j, |p| = |q| = sqrt(B) since A^2 - 4B = 4 s^2 (1-s)^2 (1 - 2 kappa)/N1^2 < 0).
18:   xi^m = X_m - i w U_m: X_0 = 1, U_0 = 0, X_{m+1} = delta X_m - w^2 U_m, U_{m+1} = X_m + delta U_m; |U_m| <= m |xi|^{m-1}.
19:   Phi/(w|xi|^2) = -sum_{j>=2} c_j U_{j-1};  Im g / w = -sum_{j>=1} c_j U_j.
20: B-edge (fixed real delta, 0 < w <= wh): Phi/w = B + w^2 C + E,  B = g(delta) - delta g'(delta),
21:   C = -g''(delta)/2 + delta g'''(delta)/6,  |E| <= wh^4 M4/24 + delta wh^4 M5/120,
22:   Im g / w >= -g'(delta) - wh^2 |g'''(delta)|/6 - wh^4 M5/120,
23:   M_k = sup |g^(k)| on R = [dl,dh] - i[0,wh]:  |g^(k)| <= (k-1)! (|v1|^k + |v2|^k + 3/|z|^k),
24:   v_{1,2} = 1/(z - z_{1,2}) = kappa s/(kappa s z - (1-s)(1 +- i c0)), c0 = sqrt(2 kappa - 1).
25: """
26: from flint import arb, acb
27: from me3jet_v1 import J, LOG, RE, IM, mvf
28: 
29: I = acb(0, 1)
30: 
31: 
32: def twopi():
33:     return 2 * arb.pi()
34: 
35: 
36: def N1f(s, kap):
37:     return kap * s * s - 2 * s * (1 - s) + 2 * (1 - s) * (1 - s)
38: 
39: 
40: def Ntf(s, z, kap):
41:     return kap * s * s * z * z - 2 * s * (1 - s) * z + 2 * (1 - s) * (1 - s)
42: 
43: 
44: def W_generic(S, Dl, Om, kap):
45:     z = 1 + Dl - I * Om
46:     return Ntf(S, z, kap) / (N1f(S, kap) * z * z * z)
47: 
48: 
49: def ball(lo, hi):
50:     return arb(lo).union(arb(hi))
51: 
52: 
53: def sym(lo, hi):
54:     h = (hi - lo) / 2
55:     return arb(-h).union(arb(h))
56: 
57: 
58: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- GENERAL chart (s, delta, w), w >= wl > 0
59: def _branch_const(W):
60:     """Return (c, mode) such that alpha = Im(log_principal(sgn*W)) + c is an argument of W, analytic on the box:
61:     mode 'up' (Im W > 0): alpha = arg W in (0, pi); 'down' (Im W < 0): arg W + 2pi in (pi, 2pi);
62:     'left' (Re W < 0): arg(-W) + pi in (pi/2, 3pi/2). None if undetermined."""
63:     if W.imag > 0:
64:         return arb(0), "up"
65:     if W.imag < 0:
66:         return twopi(), "down"
67:     if W.real < 0:
68:         return arb.pi(), "left"
69:     return None, None
70: 
71: 
72: def test_general(sl, sh, dl, dh, wl, wh, kap, theta_tri):
73:     sb, db, wb = ball(sl, sh), ball(dl, dh), ball(wl, wh)
74:     W = W_generic(acb(sb), acb(db), acb(wb), kap)
75:     aW = abs(W)
76:     # SMALL
77:     if aW < (-2 * arb.pi() * db / wb).exp():
78:         return True, "small"
79:     c, mode = _branch_const(W)
80:     if mode is not None and aW > 0:
81:         alpha = (-W).arg() + c if mode == "left" else W.arg() + c
82:         phi = wb * aW.log() + db * alpha
83:         if phi < 0:
84:             return True, "radial"
85:         # mean-value form of Phi on the box (branch fixed by mode)
86:         sgn = -1 if mode == "left" else 1
87: 
88:         def F(S, Dl, Om):
89:             Wj = W_generic(S, Dl, Om, kap)
90:             Lg = LOG(sgn * Wj)
91:             return (Om * RE(Lg) + Dl * (IM(Lg) + c),)
92:         mids = [arb((sl + sh) / 2), arb((dl + dh) / 2), arb((wl + wh) / 2)]
93:         Wm = W_generic(acb(mids[0]), acb(mids[1]), acb(mids[2]), kap)
94:         cm, modem = _branch_const(Wm)
95:         if modem == mode:
96:             (pm,) = mvf(F, mids, [sb, db, wb], [sym(sl, sh), sym(dl, dh), sym(wl, wh)])
97:             if pm < 0:
98:                 return True, "radial-mvf"
99:     # TRIANGLE with theta' (rational, in (pi, 2pi)): Z' = e^{-r theta'} e^{i theta'}, r = delta/w
100:     if theta_tri is not None:
101:         th = arb(theta_tri)
102:         rb = db / wb
103:         Zp = (-rb * th).exp() * acb(th.cos(), th.sin())
104:         if Zp.imag < 0:
105:             b = W.imag / Zp.imag
106:             a = W.real - b * Zp.real
107:             if b >= 0 and a >= 0 and a + b <= 1:
108:                 return True, "triangle"
109:     return False, "general"
110: 
111: 
112: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- LARGE-w chart (s, delta, nu = 1/w)
113: def test_largew(sl, sh, dl, dh, nl, nh, kap):
114:     sb, db, nb = ball(sl, sh), ball(dl, dh), ball(nl, nh)
115:     zz = acb(nb) / acb(nb * (1 + db), -1)
116:     N1 = N1f(sb, kap)
117:     W = (kap * sb * sb * zz - 2 * sb * (1 - sb) * zz * zz + 2 * (1 - sb) * (1 - sb) * zz * zz * zz) / N1
118:     if abs(W) < (-2 * arb.pi() * db * nb).exp():
119:         return True, "small"
120:     return False, "largew"
121: 
122: 
123: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- LARGE-delta charts
124: def _ld_ratio_small_beta(Pv, Pb, bh):
125:     """alpha = beta + arg P with arg P continuous from 0 at beta = 0 (requires Re P > 0 on the box).
126:     alpha/tan(beta) <= (1 + sup Im(P_b/P)) * (beta/tan beta), beta/tan beta in (0,1]; alpha >= 0 iff 1 + inf >= 0."""
127:     if not (Pv.real > 0):
128:         return None
129:     q = (Pb / Pv).imag
130:     lo = 1 + q
131:     if not (lo >= 0):
132:         return None
133:     if not (arb(bh) * lo.upper() < arb.pi() / 2):
134:         return None
135:     return arb(0).union(arb(lo.upper()))
136: 
137: 
138: def test_ld1(sl, sh, d0l, d0h, bl, bh, kap, beta_top):
139:     """Chart LD-1: s in [sl, sh] (sl > 0), d = 1/(1+delta) in [d0l, d0h], beta = arctan(w/(1+delta)) in [bl, bh].
140:     zeta = 1/z = d cos(b) e^{ib}; W = zeta P, P = w1 + w2 zeta + w3 zeta^2; d zeta/d beta = i d e^{2ib}."""
141:     sb, db, bb = ball(sl, sh), ball(d0l, d0h), ball(bl, bh)
142:     N1 = N1f(sb, kap)
143:     w1, w2, w3 = kap * sb * sb / N1, -2 * sb * (1 - sb) / N1, 2 * (1 - sb) * (1 - sb) / N1
144:     cb, sn = bb.cos(), bb.sin()
145:     zeta = db * cb * acb(cb, sn)
146:     P = w1 + w2 * zeta + w3 * zeta * zeta
147:     aW = abs(db * cb) * abs(P)
148:     omd = 1 - db
149:     if bh >= beta_top:
150:         if not (bl > 0 and P.real > 0):
151:             return False, "ld1-top"
152:         ratio = arb(0).union((arb.pi() / arb(bl).tan()))      # alpha = beta + arg P in [0, pi) as Re P > 0
153:     elif bl > 0:
154:         if not (P.real > 0):
155:             return False, "ld1-ReP"
156:         alpha = bb + P.arg()
157:         if not (alpha >= 0):
158:             return False, "ld1-alpha"
159:         ratio = alpha / bb.tan()
160:     else:
161:         e2 = acb((2 * bb).cos(), (2 * bb).sin())
162:         Pb = (w2 + 2 * w3 * zeta) * I * db * e2
163:         ratio = _ld_ratio_small_beta(P, Pb, bh)
164:         if ratio is None:
165:             return False, "ld1-ratio"
166:     if aW < (-omd * ratio).exp():
167:         return True, "ld1"
168:     return False, "ld1"
169: 
170: 
171: def test_ld2(xl, xh, ql, qh, bl, bh, kap, beta_top, s_max, d_max):
172:     """Chart LD-2 (blow-up at s, zeta -> 0): s = chi*rt, |zeta| = d cos b = (1-chi)*rt, chi in [xl,xh], rt in [ql,qh].
173:     P-hat = P/rt^2 = [kappa chi^2 - 2 chi (1-chi)(1-s) e^{ib} + 2 (1-s)^2 (1-chi)^2 e^{2ib}]/N1(s).
174:     W = (1-chi) rt^3 e^{ib} P-hat. Feasibility: s <= s_max and d = (1-chi) rt/cos b <= d_max.
175:     Returns 'skip' when the whole box is infeasible."""
176:     xb, qb, bb = ball(xl, xh), ball(ql, qh), ball(bl, bh)
177:     # infeasible-box skips (exact corner tests)
178:     if arb(xl) * arb(ql) > arb(s_max):
179:         return True, "skip-s"
180:     if (1 - arb(xh)) * arb(ql) > arb(d_max) * arb(bl).cos():
181:         return True, "skip-d"
182:     sb = xb * qb
183:     N1 = N1f(sb, kap)
184:     eb = acb(bb.cos(), bb.sin())
185:     e2b = eb * eb
186:     Ph = (kap * xb * xb - 2 * xb * (1 - xb) * (1 - sb) * eb + 2 * (1 - sb) * (1 - sb) * (1 - xb) * (1 - xb) * e2b) / N1
187:     aW = abs(1 - xb) * qb * qb * qb * abs(Ph)
188:     omd = 1 - arb(d_max)          # 1 - d >= 1 - d_max for every feasible point
189:     if bh >= beta_top:
190:         if not (bl > 0 and Ph.real > 0):
191:             return False, "ld2-top"
192:         ratio = arb(0).union((arb.pi() / arb(bl).tan()))
193:     elif bl > 0:
194:         if not (Ph.real > 0):
195:             return False, "ld2-ReP"
196:         alpha = bb + Ph.arg()
197:         if not (alpha >= 0):
198:             return False, "ld2-alpha"
199:         ratio = alpha / bb.tan()
200:     else:
201:         Pb = (-2 * xb * (1 - xb) * (1 - sb) * I * eb + 4 * (1 - sb) * (1 - sb) * (1 - xb) * (1 - xb) * I * e2b) / N1
202:         ratio = _ld_ratio_small_beta(Ph, Pb, bh)
203:         if ratio is None:
204:             return False, "ld2-ratio"
205:     if aW < (-omd * ratio).exp():
206:         return True, "ld2"
207:     return False, "ld2"
208: 
209: 
210: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- SMALL-w corner chart (series)
211: def corner_series_generic(S, Dl, Om, kap, N):
212:     """Truncated Psi_N = -sum_{j=2}^N c_j U_{j-1} and IgN = -sum_{j=1}^N c_j U_j (generic over acb/jets)."""
213:     N1 = N1f(S, kap)
214:     A = (2 * kap * S * S - 2 * S * (1 - S)) / N1
215:     B = kap * S * S / N1
216:     Pm2, Pm1 = 2, -A
217:     c = [None, -Pm1 / 1 + 3 * (-1) / 1]
218:     for j in range(2, N + 1):
219:         Pj = -A * Pm1 - B * Pm2
220:         c.append(-Pj / j + 3 * (-1) ** j / j)
221:         Pm2, Pm1 = Pm1, Pj
222:     X, U = [1], [0]
223:     w2 = Om * Om
224:     for m in range(N):
225:         X.append(Dl * X[m] - w2 * U[m])
226:         U.append(X[m] + Dl * U[m])
227:     psi = 0
228:     for j in range(2, N + 1):
229:         psi = psi - c[j] * U[j - 1]
230:     ig = 0
231:     for j in range(1, N + 1):
232:         ig = ig - c[j] * U[j]
233:     return psi, ig
234: 
235: 
236: def test_corner(sl, sh, dl, dh, wl, wh, kap, N):
237:     sb, db, wb = ball(sl, sh), ball(dl, dh), ball(wl, wh)
238:     N1 = N1f(sb, kap)
239:     Bup = arb((kap * sb * sb / N1).upper())
240:     rp = Bup.sqrt()
241:     xm = (arb(dh) * arb(dh) + arb(wh) * arb(wh)).sqrt()
242:     q1 = rp * xm
243:     if not (q1 < 1 and xm < 1):
244:         return False, "corner-radius"
245:     tail_psi = 2 * rp * rp * q1 ** (N - 1) / (1 - q1) + 3 * xm ** (N - 1) / (1 - xm)
246:     tail_ig = 2 * rp * q1 ** N / (1 - q1) + 3 * xm ** N / (1 - xm)
247:     psi, ig = corner_series_generic(acb(sb), acb(db), acb(wb), kap, N)
248:     psi, ig = psi.real, ig.real
249:     if not (ig - tail_ig >= 0):
250:         # mean-value form for ig
251:         def Fi(S, Dl, Om):
252:             p_, i_ = corner_series_generic(S, Dl, Om, kap, N)
253:             return (i_,)
254:         mids = [arb((sl + sh) / 2), arb((dl + dh) / 2), arb((wl + wh) / 2)]
255:         (ig,) = mvf(Fi, mids, [sb, db, wb], [sym(sl, sh), sym(dl, dh), sym(wl, wh)])
256:         if not (ig - tail_ig >= 0):
257:             return False, "corner-img"
258:     if psi + tail_psi < 0:
259:         return True, "corner"
260: 
261:     def Fp(S, Dl, Om):
262:         p_, i_ = corner_series_generic(S, Dl, Om, kap, N)
263:         return (p_,)
264:     mids = [arb((sl + sh) / 2), arb((dl + dh) / 2), arb((wl + wh) / 2)]
265:     (pm,) = mvf(Fp, mids, [sb, db, wb], [sym(sl, sh), sym(dl, dh), sym(wl, wh)])
266:     if pm + tail_psi < 0:
267:         return True, "corner-mvf"
268:     return False, "corner"
269: 
270: 
271: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- SMALL-w B-edge chart (expansion in w)
272: def gk_real_generic(S, Dl, kap):
273:     """g, g', g'', g''' at real xi = delta (generic over acb/jets; inputs real-valued)."""
274:     z = 1 + Dl
275:     N1 = N1f(S, kap)
276:     Nt = Ntf(S, z, kap)
277:     Nz = 2 * kap * S * S * z - 2 * S * (1 - S)
278:     Nzz = 2 * kap * S * S
279:     g0 = LOG(Nt / N1) - 3 * LOG(z)
280:     g1 = Nz / Nt - 3 / z
281:     g2 = (Nzz * Nt - Nz * Nz) / (Nt * Nt) + 3 / (z * z)
282:     g3 = (2 * Nz * Nz * Nz - 3 * Nt * Nz * Nzz) / (Nt * Nt * Nt) - 6 / (z * z * z)
283:     return g0, g1, g2, g3
284: 
285: 
286: def test_bedge(sl, sh, dl, dh, wh, kap):
287:     sb, db = ball(sl, sh), ball(dl, dh)
288:     whb = arb(wh)
289:     # bounds M4, M5 on R = [dl,dh] - i[0,wh]
290:     c0 = (2 * kap - 1).sqrt()
291:     zR = acb(1 + db, -(arb(0).union(whb)))
292:     v1 = kap * sb / (kap * sb * zR - (1 - sb) * acb(1, c0))
293:     v2 = kap * sb / (kap * sb * zR - (1 - sb) * acb(1, -c0))
294:     a1, a2, az = abs(v1), abs(v2), 1 / abs(zR)
295:     M4 = 6 * (a1 ** 4 + a2 ** 4 + 3 * az ** 4)
296:     M5 = 24 * (a1 ** 5 + a2 ** 5 + 3 * az ** 5)
297:     if not (M4.is_finite() and M5.is_finite()):
298:         return False, "bedge-M"
299:     w4 = whb ** 4
300:     E = w4 * M4 / 24 + arb(dh) * w4 * M5 / 120
301:     g0, g1, g2, g3 = gk_real_generic(acb(sb), acb(db), kap)
302:     g0, g1, g2, g3 = g0.real, g1.real, g2.real, g3.real
303:     # Im g >= 0 condition
304:     imlow = -g1 - whb * whb * abs(g3) / 6 - w4 * M5 / 120
305:     if not (imlow > 0):
306:         return False, "bedge-img"
307:     Bv = g0 - db * g1
308:     Cv = -g2 / 2 + db * g3 / 6
309:     Cp = arb(0).union(Cv) if not (Cv < 0) else arb(0)
310:     Cp = arb(Cp.upper()) if Cp.upper() > 0 else arb(0)
311:     if Bv + whb * whb * Cp + E < 0:
312:         return True, "bedge"
313: 
314:     def Fb(S, Dl, Om):
315:         a, b, c, d = gk_real_generic(S, Dl, kap)
316:         return (a - Dl * b, -c / 2 + Dl * d / 6)
317:     mids = [arb((sl + sh) / 2), arb((dl + dh) / 2), arb(0)]
318:     Bm, Cm = mvf(Fb, mids, [sb, db, arb(0)], [sym(sl, sh), sym(dl, dh), arb(0)])
319:     Cp2 = arb(Cm.upper()) if Cm.upper() > 0 else arb(0)
320:     if Bm + whb * whb * Cp2 + E < 0:
321:         return True, "bedge-mvf"
322:     return False, "bedge"

1: """DUAL far-region tests, version 4 (supersedes me3dual_v3.py for certified use; v3 kept unchanged).
2: 
3: Changes from v3 (found in exploratory calibration k02, run x05-debugG):
4:   * W is evaluated as W = w1 zz + w2 zz^2 + w3 zz^3 with zz = 1/(1+xi) and w1 = kappa s^2/N1, w2 = -2 s(1-s)/N1,
5:     w3 = 2(1-s)^2/N1 (identical to Nt(s,1+xi)/(N1 (1+xi)^3), but without the cancellation of the three O(1) terms
6:     of Nt that made the naive ball of W contain 0 for small s and large |xi|);
7:   * test_general first encloses W by the complex mean-value form W(m) + sum_j dW_j(B)[-h_j, h_j] (valid: W(x) - W(m)
8:     is an integral of grad W over the segment, whose values lie in the convex balls dW_j(B)), and uses the
9:     intersection with the naive ball for the branch decision, the SMALL test and the RADIAL test;
10:   * LD top boxes (beta reaching pi/2) use Arg_+ in [0, 2 pi): ratio <= 2 pi / tan(beta_lo) (v3 wrongly assumed
11:     beta + arg P >= 0 there).
12: All other definitions, certificates and identities are as documented in me3dual_v3.py (LEMMA D, series, B-edge).
13: """
14: from flint import arb, acb
15: from me3jet_v1 import J, LOG, RE, IM, mvf
16: import me3dual_v3 as V3
17: 
18: I = acb(0, 1)
19: ball, sym, N1f = V3.ball, V3.sym, V3.N1f
20: 
21: 
22: def twopi():
23:     return 2 * arb.pi()
24: 
25: 
26: def W_generic(S, Dl, Om, kap):
27:     N1 = N1f(S, kap)
28:     zz = 1 / (1 + Dl - I * Om)
29:     w1 = kap * S * S / N1
30:     w2 = -2 * S * (1 - S) / N1
31:     w3 = 2 * (1 - S) * (1 - S) / N1
32:     return zz * (w1 + zz * (w2 + zz * w3))
33: 
34: 
35: def W_enclosure(sl, sh, dl, dh, wl, wh, kap):
36:     sb, db, wb = ball(sl, sh), ball(dl, dh), ball(wl, wh)
37:     Wn = W_generic(acb(sb), acb(db), acb(wb), kap)
38:     mids = [arb((sl + sh) / 2), arb((dl + dh) / 2), arb((wl + wh) / 2)]
39:     hb = [sym(sl, sh), sym(dl, dh), sym(wl, wh)]
40:     from me3jet_v1 import jet_inputs
41:     Wm = W_generic(*[acb(m) for m in mids], kap)
42:     Wj = W_generic(*jet_inputs([sb, db, wb]), kap)
43:     re = Wm.real
44:     im = Wm.imag
45:     for j in range(3):
46:         re = re + Wj.d[j].real * hb[j]
47:         im = im + Wj.d[j].imag * hb[j]
48:     # intersect with the naive enclosure when both are finite
49:     rr = re.intersection(Wn.real) if Wn.real.is_finite() else re
50:     ii = im.intersection(Wn.imag) if Wn.imag.is_finite() else im
51:     return acb(rr, ii), sb, db, wb, mids, hb
52: 
53: 
54: def _branch(W):
55:     if W.imag > 0:
56:         return arb(0), "up", 1
57:     if W.imag < 0:
58:         return twopi(), "down", 1
59:     if W.real < 0:
60:         return arb.pi(), "left", -1
61:     return None, None, None

64: def test_general(sl, sh, dl, dh, wl, wh, kap, theta_tri):
65:     W, sb, db, wb, mids, hb = W_enclosure(sl, sh, dl, dh, wl, wh, kap)

72:         phi = wb * aW.log() + db * alpha
73:         if phi < 0:
74:             return True, "radial"
75:         Wm = W_generic(*[acb(m) for m in mids], kap)
76:         cm, modem, _ = _branch(Wm)
77:         if modem == mode:

79:                 Lg = LOG(sgn * W_generic(S, Dl, Om, kap))
80:                 return (Om * RE(Lg) + Dl * (IM(Lg) + c),)

82:             if pm < 0:

86:         rb = db / wb
87:         Zp = (-rb * th).exp() * acb(th.cos(), th.sin())
88:         if Zp.imag < 0:
89:             b = W.imag / Zp.imag
90:             a = W.real - b * Zp.real
91:             if b >= 0 and a >= 0 and a + b <= 1:
92:                 return True, "triangle"
93:     return False, "general"

91: def gk_real(S, Dl, kap):
92:     """g, g', g'', g''' at real xi = delta (S, Dl real arb balls or real jets)."""

95:     Nt = kap * S * S * z * z - 2 * S * (1 - S) * z + 2 * (1 - S) * (1 - S)
96:     Nz = 2 * kap * S * S * z - 2 * S * (1 - S)
97:     Nzz = 2 * kap * S * S
98:     g0 = LOG(Nt / N1) - 3 * LOG(z)

100:     g2 = (Nzz * Nt - Nz * Nz) / (Nt * Nt) + 3 / (z * z)
101:     g3 = (2 * Nz * Nz * Nz - 3 * Nt * Nz * Nzz) / (Nt * Nt * Nt) - 6 / (z * z * z)

145:             return True, "bedge"

SOURCE 17-me3dual_v7.py SHA256 b2f80d148575bbd3bb590f753578d5b60269572d7664335df6fa798072a91895 ORIGINAL /Users/jinleic/repos/aifours/mathematics/evidence/six-lane-20261016/me3-minimum-scv/code/me3dual_v7.py
1: """DUAL far-region tests, version 7 (for certified use): me3dual_v6.py with a numerically stable CORNER series.
2: 
3: Change (found in exploratory calibration k03-cal-SWC): v3/v6 computed the power sums P_j = p^j + q^j by the linear
4: recurrence P_j = -A P_{j-1} - B P_{j-2}, whose ball enclosure inflates like ((|A| + B)/sqrt(B))^j. v7 computes
5: P_j = 2 Re(p^j) with p = (-A + i sqrt(4B - A^2))/2 by repeated complex multiplication (stable), both in the naive
6: ball evaluation and in the mean-value form (jets with SQRT, me3jet_v2). Identity: x^2 + A x + B = (x - p)(x - q),
7: q = conj(p), since 4B - A^2 = 4 s^2 (1-s)^2 (2 kappa - 1)/N1^2 >= 0 (> 0 for 0 < s < 1; at s in {0, 1} p = q real
8: and P_j = 2 p^j still holds). Then 1 + A xi + B xi^2 = (1 - p xi)(1 - q xi)... NOTE the series of
9: log(1 + A xi + B xi^2) is -sum_j (p'^j + q'^j) xi^j / j with p', q' the reciprocals' negatives: we need the roots of
10: y^2 + A y + B in the form (1 - p' xi)(1 - q' xi) = 1 - (p'+q') xi + p'q' xi^2, i.e. p' + q' = -A, p' q' = B, which are
11: exactly the roots of x^2 + A x + B = 0. So P_j = p'^j + q'^j with p' = (-A + i sqrt(4B - A^2))/2 (and conj), |p'| =
12: sqrt(B). All other tests are those of me3dual_v6.py.
13: """
14: from flint import arb, acb
15: from me3jet_v1 import J, RE, mvf
16: from me3jet_v2 import SQRT
17: import me3dual_v3 as V3
18: import me3dual_v6 as V6
19: 
20: I = acb(0, 1)
21: ball, sym, N1f = V3.ball, V3.sym, V3.N1f
22: test_general, test_largew, test_ld1, test_ld2, test_bedge = (V6.test_general, V6.test_largew, V6.test_ld1,
23:                                                              V6.test_ld2, V6.test_bedge)

26: def corner_series_stable(S, Dl, Om, kap, N):
27:     """Psi_N = -sum_{j=2}^N c_j U_{j-1} and IgN = -sum_{j=1}^N c_j U_j with c_j = -2 Re(p^j)/j + 3(-1)^j/j."""
28:     N1 = N1f(S, kap)
29:     A = (2 * kap * S * S - 2 * S * (1 - S)) / N1
30:     B = kap * S * S / N1
31:     p = (-A + I * SQRT(4 * B - A * A)) / 2
32:     c = [None]
33:     pk = p
34:     for j in range(1, N + 1):
35:         c.append(-2 * RE(pk) / j + 3 * (-1) ** j / j)
36:         pk = pk * p
37:     X, U = [1], [0]
38:     w2 = Om * Om
39:     for m in range(N):
40:         X.append(Dl * X[m] - w2 * U[m])
41:         U.append(X[m] + Dl * U[m])
42:     psi = 0
43:     for j in range(2, N + 1):
44:         psi = psi - c[j] * U[j - 1]
45:     ig = 0
46:     for j in range(1, N + 1):
47:         ig = ig - c[j] * U[j]
48:     return psi, ig
49:
